[EVENT: YEM109901]
Date: 2023-10-11
Country: Yemen | Location: Coast of Al Fazzah
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Military Forces of Yemen (2017-) Houthi [State forces]
Actor2: Military Forces of Yemen (2022-2026) Presidential Leadership Council [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Undisclosed Source [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 11 October 2023, Houthi flagged dhow clashed with a Joint Forces-flagged transport boat (coded as IRG forces) of the Coast of Al Fazzah (At Tuhayat, Al Hudaydah) after the Houthi boat carrying weapons passed near the Joint Forces-flagged transport boat heading to Zuqar Island. The vessels exchanged fire briefly before the Houthi boat withdrew to the Al Matinah coast, while the Joint Forces vessel continued to its destination. The Houthi boat later docked at a military port on the Al Fazzah coast. There were no casualties.

DESCRIPTION:

Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Yemen) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Ansar Allah [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 24 October 2023, students held a Houthi-sponsored protest in Hajjah City district (Hajjah) in solidarity with the Palestinian people and the Hamas 'Al Aqsa Flood' Operation and to denounce Israeli 'crimes' in the context of the latest round of violence in Israel-Palestine.

[EVENT: YEM83237]
Date: 2023-10-24
Country: Yemen | Location: Aden - Ash Shaikh Outhman
Type: Riots > Violent demonstration | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Rioters (Yemen) [Rioters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Aden al Ghad; Twitter; Yemen Data Project [Local partner-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 24 October 2023, demonstrators closed the road with burning tires in the districts of Al Mansura (coded to Aden - Al Mansurah) and Ash Shaikh Outhman (coded to Aden - Ash Shaikh Outhman) against continued electricity cuts and to denounce the government's refusal to provide Aden electricity with fuel.
